===== M0003 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 530-572
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
530 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
531 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
532 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
533 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
534 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
535 |     if (objectType == null) {
536 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
537 |     } else {
538 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
539 |         return;
540 |       }
541 | 
542 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
543 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
544 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
545 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
546 |         //
547 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
548 |         //    inferred type.
549 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
550 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
551 |         //    define it.
552 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
553 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
554 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
555 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
556 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
557 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
558 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
559 |           } else {
560 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
561 |           }
562 |         } else {
563 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
564 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
565 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
566 |           } else {
567 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
568 |           }
569 |         }
570 |       }
571 |     }
572 |   }

===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 361-376
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
361 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
362 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
363 |       Set<String> directives = null;
364 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
365 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
366 |         if (directives == null) {
367 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
368 |         } else {
369 |           directives.add(directive);
370 |         }
371 |       }
372 | 
373 |       if (directives != null) {
374 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
375 |       }
376 |     }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 821-848
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
821 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
822 |     // unknown
823 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
824 |       return true;
825 |     }
826 |     // equality
827 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
828 |       return true;
829 |     }
830 |     // all type
831 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
832 |       return true;
833 |     }
834 |     // unions
835 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
836 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
837 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
838 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
839 |           return true;
840 |         }
841 |       }
842 |     }
843 |     // named types
844 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
845 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
846 |     }
847 |     return false;
848 |   }

/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
888 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
889 |     switch (n.getType()) {
890 |       case Token.CALL:
891 |       case Token.GETPROP:
892 |       case Token.GETELEM:
893 |       case Token.THROW:
894 |       case Token.NEW:
895 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
896 |       case Token.INC:
897 |       case Token.DEC:
898 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
899 |         return true;
900 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
901 |         return false;
902 |     }
903 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
904 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
905 |         return true;
906 |       }
907 |     }
908 |     return false;
909 |   }

===== M0032 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 288-448

Method code:
288 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
289 |     switch (n.getType()) {
290 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
291 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
292 |         break;
293 | 
294 |       case Token.NAME:
295 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
296 |         break;
297 | 
298 |       case Token.GETPROP:
299 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
300 |         break;
301 | 
302 |       case Token.AND:
303 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
304 |             .createChildFlowScope();
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.OR:
308 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
309 |             .createChildFlowScope();
310 |         break;
311 | 
312 |       case Token.HOOK:
313 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
314 |         break;
315 | 
316 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
317 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
318 |         break;
319 | 
320 |       case Token.CALL:
321 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
322 |         break;
323 | 
324 |       case Token.NEW:
325 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
326 |         break;
327 | 
328 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
329 |       case Token.ADD:
330 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
331 |         break;
332 | 
333 |       case Token.POS:
334 |       case Token.NEG:
335 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
336 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
337 |         break;
338 | 
339 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
340 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
341 |         break;
342 | 
343 |       case Token.THIS:
344 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
345 |         break;
346 | 
347 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
348 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
349 |       case Token.LSH:
350 |       case Token.RSH:
351 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
352 |       case Token.URSH:
353 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
354 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
355 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
356 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
357 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
358 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
359 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
360 |       case Token.DIV:
361 |       case Token.MOD:
362 |       case Token.BITAND:
363 |       case Token.BITXOR:
364 |       case Token.BITOR:
365 |       case Token.MUL:
366 |       case Token.SUB:
367 |       case Token.DEC:
368 |       case Token.INC:
369 |       case Token.BITNOT:
370 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
371 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
372 |         break;
373 | 
374 |       case Token.LP:
375 |       case Token.GET_REF:
376 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
377 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
378 |         break;
379 | 
380 |       case Token.COMMA:
381 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
382 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
383 |         break;
384 | 
385 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
386 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
387 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
388 |         break;
389 | 
390 |       case Token.LT:
391 |       case Token.LE:
392 |       case Token.GT:
393 |       case Token.GE:
394 |       case Token.NOT:
395 |       case Token.EQ:
396 |       case Token.NE:
397 |       case Token.SHEQ:
398 |       case Token.SHNE:
399 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
400 |       case Token.IN:
401 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
402 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
403 |         break;
404 | 
405 |       case Token.GETELEM:
406 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
407 |         break;
408 | 
409 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
410 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
411 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
412 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
413 |         }
414 |         break;
415 | 
416 |       case Token.SWITCH:
417 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
418 |         break;
419 | 
420 |       case Token.VAR:
421 |       case Token.RETURN:
422 |       case Token.THROW:
423 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
424 |         break;
425 | 
426 |       case Token.CATCH:
427 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
428 |         break;
429 |     }
430 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
431 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
432 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
433 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
434 | 
435 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
436 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
437 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
438 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
439 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
440 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
441 |         }
442 | 
443 |         n.setJSType(castType);
444 |       }
445 |     }
446 | 
447 |     return scope;
448 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 870-953
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
870 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
871 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
872 | 
873 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
874 |       return;
875 |     }
876 | 
877 |     int i = 0;
878 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
879 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
880 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
881 |       JSType iParameterType =
882 |           getJSType(iParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
883 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
884 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
885 |         JSType iArgumentType = null;
886 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeArgument = false;
887 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
888 |           foundTemplateTypeArgument = true;
889 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
890 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
891 |           if (!(iArgumentType instanceof ObjectType)) {
892 |             compiler.report(
893 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
894 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE));
895 |             return;
896 |           }
897 |         }
898 | 
899 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
900 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
901 |         int j = 0;
902 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
903 |           JSType jParameterType =
904 |               getJSType(jParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
905 |           if (jParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
906 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = (FunctionType) jParameterType;
907 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
908 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
909 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
910 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
911 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
912 |                 return;
913 |               }
914 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
915 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
916 |               if (jArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
917 |                   jArgumentType instanceof FunctionType) {
918 |                 if (foundTemplateTypeArgument) {
919 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
920 |                   // using the actual type of T.
921 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = (FunctionType) jArgumentType;
922 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
923 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
924 |                     // function.
925 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
926 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
927 |                             jArgumentFnType, (ObjectType) iArgumentType));
928 |                   }
929 |                 } else {
930 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
931 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
932 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
933 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
934 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
935 |                   }
936 |                 }
937 |               }
938 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
939 |               // types of the arguments match.
940 |             }
941 |           }
942 |           j++;
943 |         }
944 | 
945 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
946 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
947 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
948 |           return;
949 |         }
950 |       }
951 |       i++;
952 |     }
953 |   }

771 |   private FlowScope traverseCall(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
772 |     scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
773 | 
774 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
775 |     JSType functionType = getJSType(left).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
776 |     if (functionType != null) {

   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.

Method code:
1253 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1254 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1255 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1256 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1257 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1258 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1259 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1260 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1261 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1262 |       case Token.LABEL:
1263 |         return true;
1264 |       default:
1265 |         return false;
1266 |     }
1267 |   }

Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 49-86
Documentation:

Method code:
49 |   @Override
50 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
51 |     Node node = error.node;
52 |     if (node != null) {
53 |       for (Node current = node;
54 |            current != null;
55 |            current = current.getParent()) {
56 |         int type = current.getType();
57 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
58 | 
59 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
60 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
61 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
62 |         // a bad idea.
63 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
64 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(current);
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         }
68 | 
69 |         if (info != null) {
70 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
71 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
72 | 
73 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
74 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
75 |             if (guard != null) {
76 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
77 |               if (newLevel != null) {
78 |                 return newLevel;
79 |               }
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 |     return null;
86 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1860-1899
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a new union type, with an alternate that has already been
   * parsed. The alternate may be null.
   */
Method code:
1860 |   private Node parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken token, Node alternate) {
1861 |     Node union = newNode(Token.PIPE);
1862 |     if (alternate != null) {
1863 |       union.addChildToBack(alternate);
1864 |     }
1865 | 
1866 |     Node expr = null;
1867 |     do {
1868 |       if (expr != null) {
1869 |         skipEOLs();
1870 |         token = next();
1871 |         Preconditions.checkState(
1872 |             token == JsDocToken.PIPE || token == JsDocToken.COMMA);
1873 | 
1874 |         boolean isPipe = token == JsDocToken.PIPE;
1875 |         if (isPipe && match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1876 |           // We support double pipes for backwards compatiblity.
1877 |           next();
1878 |         }
1879 |         skipEOLs();
1880 |         token = next();
1881 |       }
1882 |       expr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1883 |       if (expr == null) {
1884 |         return null;
1885 |       }
1886 | 
1887 |       union.addChildToBack(expr);
1888 |       // We support commas for backwards compatiblity.
1889 |     } while (match(JsDocToken.PIPE, JsDocToken.COMMA));
1890 | 
1891 |     if (alternate == null) {
1892 |       skipEOLs();
1893 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
1894 |         return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rp");
1895 |       }
1896 |       next();
1897 |     }
1898 |     return union;
1899 |   }

===== M0093 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getFunctionType(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 745-822
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the function type from the function node and its attached
     * {@link JSDocInfo}.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param rValue the function node. It must be a {@link Token#FUNCTION}.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
745 |     private FunctionType getFunctionType(String name,
746 |         Node rValue, JSDocInfo info, @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
747 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
748 | 
749 |       // Global function aliases should be registered with the type registry.
750 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
751 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
752 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
753 |           functionType = (FunctionType) var.getType();
754 |           if (functionType != null && functionType.isConstructor()) {
755 |             typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
756 |           }
757 |         }
758 |         return functionType;
759 |       }
760 | 
761 |       Node owner = null;
762 |       if (lvalueNode != null) {
763 |         owner = getPrototypePropertyOwner(lvalueNode);
764 |       }
765 | 
766 |       Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
767 |       boolean isFnLiteral =
768 |           rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
769 |       Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
770 |       Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
771 |           rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
772 |       Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
773 | 
774 |       if (functionType == null && info != null && info.hasType()) {
775 |         JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
776 | 
777 |         // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
778 |         type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
779 |         if (type.isFunctionType()) {
780 |           functionType = (FunctionType) type;
781 |           functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
782 |         }
783 |       }
784 | 
785 |       if (functionType == null) {
786 |         // Find the type of any overridden function.
787 |         FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
788 |         if (lvalueNode != null && lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
789 |             lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
790 |           Var var = scope.getVar(
791 |               lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
792 |           if (var != null) {
793 |             ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
794 |             if (ownerType != null) {
795 |               String propName = lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString();
796 |               overriddenPropType = findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
797 |             }
798 |           }
799 |         }
800 | 
801 |         functionType =
802 |             new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
803 |                 scope)
804 |             .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
805 |             .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
806 |             .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
807 |             .inferReturnType(info)
808 |             .inferInheritance(info)
809 |             .inferThisType(info, owner)
810 |             .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
811 |             .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
812 |             .buildAndRegister();
813 |       }
814 | 
815 |       // assigning the function type to the function node
816 |       if (rValue != null) {
817 |         setDeferredType(rValue, functionType);
818 |       }
819 | 
820 |       // all done
821 |       return functionType;
822 |     }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 137-873
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
137 |   boolean parse() {
138 |     int lineno;
139 |     int charno;
140 | 
141 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
142 |     JSTypeExpression type;
143 | 
144 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
145 |     skipEOLs();
146 | 
147 |     JsDocToken token = next();
148 | 
149 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
150 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
151 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
152 |       token = blockInfo.token;
153 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
154 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
155 |       }
156 |     } else {
157 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
158 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
159 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
160 |         // what it was.
161 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
162 |       }
163 |     }
164 | 
165 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
166 |     retry: for (;;) {
167 |       switch (token) {
168 |         case ANNOTATION:
169 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
170 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
171 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
172 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
173 | 
174 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
175 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
176 |             if (annotation == null) {
177 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
178 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
179 |             } else {
180 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
181 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
182 | 
183 |               switch (annotation) {
184 |                 case AUTHOR:
185 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
186 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
187 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
188 |

===== M0111 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(ObjectType,JSDocInfo,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 190-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Handle cases #1 and #3 in the class doc.
   */
Method code:
190 |   private void attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
191 |       ObjectType objType, JSDocInfo docInfo, @Nullable String qName) {
192 |     if (objType.isConstructor() ||
193 |         objType.isEnumType() ||
194 |         objType.isInterface()) {
195 |       // Named types.
196 |       if (objType.hasReferenceName() &&
197 |           objType.getReferenceName().equals(qName)) {
198 |         objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
199 | 
200 |         if (objType.isConstructor() || objType.isInterface()) {
201 |           ((FunctionType) objType).getInstanceType().setJSDocInfo(
202 |               docInfo);
203 |         } else if (objType instanceof EnumType) {
204 |           ((EnumType) objType).getElementsType().setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
205 |         }
206 |       }
207 |     } else if (!objType.isNativeObjectType() &&
208 |         objType.isFunctionType()) {
209 |       // Structural functions.
210 |       objType.setJSDocInfo(docInfo);
211 |     }
212 |   }

===== M0127 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.LocalScopeBuilder.handleFunctionInputs(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1547-1566
Documentation:
/** Handle bleeding functions and function parameters. */
Method code:
1547 |     private void handleFunctionInputs(Node fnNode) {
1548 |       // Handle bleeding functions.
1549 |       Node fnNameNode = fnNode.getFirstChild();
1550 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
1551 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty()) {
1552 |         Scope.Var fnVar = scope.getVar(fnName);
1553 |         if (fnVar == null ||
1554 |             // Make sure we're not touching a native function. Native
1555 |             // functions aren't bleeding, but may not have a declaration
1556 |             // node.
1557 |             (fnVar.getNameNode() != null &&
1558 |                 // Make sure that the function is actually bleeding by checking
1559 |                 // if has already been declared.
1560 |                 fnVar.getInitialValue() != fnNode)) {
1561 |           defineSlot(fnNameNode, fnNode, fnNode.getJSType(), false);
1562 |         }
1563 |       }
1564 | 
1565 |       declareArguments(fnNode);
1566 |     }

===== M0131 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 271-293
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
271 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
272 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(n);
273 | 
274 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
275 |       // which are var_args.
276 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
277 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
278 |            arg != null;
279 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
280 |         String argName = arg.getString();
281 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
282 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
283 | 
284 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
285 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
286 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
287 |         }
288 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
289 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
290 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
291 |         }
292 |       }
293 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 306-337
Documentation:
/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
306 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
307 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
308 |     if (functionBlock == null || compiler.getInput(sourceName).isExtern()) {
309 |       return this;
310 |     }
311 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
312 |     if (returnType == null) {
313 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
314 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
315 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
316 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
317 |         int cType = current.getType();
318 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null ||
319 |             cType == Token.THROW) {
320 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
321 |           break;
322 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
323 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
324 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
325 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
326 |             worklist.add(child);
327 |           }
328 |         }
329 |       }
330 | 
331 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
332 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
333 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
334 |       }
335 |     }
336 |     return this;
337 |   }

===== M0158 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 588-614
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
588 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
589 |     ObjectType owner = null;
590 | 
591 |     // Properties can only be defined on object types, so normalize everything
592 |     // to discrete object types.
593 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType) {
594 |       owner = (ObjectType) type;
595 |     } else if (getNativeType(ALL_TYPE).isSubtype(type)) {
596 |       owner = getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE);
597 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
598 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
599 |         registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, alternate);
600 |       }
601 |     }
602 | 
603 |     if (owner == null) {
604 |       return;
605 |     }
606 | 
607 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
608 |     if (typeSet == null) {
609 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(
610 |           propertyName, typeSet = new LinkedHashSet<ObjectType>());
611 |     }
612 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
613 |     typeSet.add(owner);
614 |   }

===== M0161 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 670-767
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
670 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
671 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
672 |     /*
673 |      * This is the case where:
674 |      *
675 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
676 |      * the script.
677 |      *
678 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
679 |      * the caller of the function.
680 |      *
681 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
682 |      * back to the caller of the function.
683 |      *
684 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
685 |      * asked to traverse.
686 |      *
687 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
688 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
689 |      */
690 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
691 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
692 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
693 |       return null;
694 |     }
695 | 
696 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
697 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
698 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
699 |       case Token.IF:
700 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
703 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
704 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
705 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
706 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
707 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
708 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
709 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
710 |           } else {
711 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
712 |           }
713 |         } else {
714 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
715 |         }
716 |         break;
717 |       case Token.FOR:
718 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
719 |           return parent;
720 |         } else {
721 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
722 |         }
723 |       case Token.WHILE:
724 |       case Token.DO:
725 |         return parent;
726 |       case Token.TRY:
727 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
728 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
729 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
730 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
731 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
732 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
733 |           }
734 |         // CATCH block.
735 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
736 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
737 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
738 |           } else {
739 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
740 |           }
741 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
742 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
743 |           if (cfa != null) {
744 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
745 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
746 |             }
747 |           }
748 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
749 |         }
750 |     }
751 | 
752 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
753 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
754 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
755 | 
756 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
757 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
758 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
759 |     }
760 | 
761 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
762 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
763 |     } else {
764 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
765 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
766 |     }
767 |   }

/**

Method code:

===== M0179 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 555-569
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
558 |       return scope;
559 |     }
560 | 
561 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
562 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
563 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
564 |       scopes.push(scope);
565 |     }
566 |     scopeRoots.clear();
567 | 
568 |     return scope;
569 |   }

===== M0180 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.GlobalScopeBuilder.checkForTypedef(NodeTraversal,Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1443-1471
Documentation:
/**
     * Handle typedefs.
     * @param t The current traversal.
     * @param candidate A qualified name node.
     * @param info JSDoc comments.
     */
Method code:
1443 |     private void checkForTypedef(
1444 |         NodeTraversal t, Node candidate, JSDocInfo info) {
1445 |       if (info == null || !info.hasTypedefType()) {
1446 |         return;
1447 |       }
1448 | 
1449 |       String typedef = candidate.getQualifiedName();
1450 |       if (typedef == null) {
1451 |         return;
1452 |       }
1453 | 
1454 |       // TODO(nicksantos|user): This is a terrible, terrible hack
1455 |       // to bail out on recusive typedefs. We'll eventually need
1456 |       // to handle these properly.
1457 |       typeRegistry.declareType(typedef, getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1458 | 
1459 |       JSType realType = info.getTypedefType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
1460 |       if (realType == null) {
1461 |         compiler.report(
1462 |             JSError.make(
1463 |                 t.getSourceName(), candidate, MALFORMED_TYPEDEF, typedef));
1464 |       }
1465 | 
1466 |       typeRegistry.overwriteDeclaredType(typedef, realType);
1467 |       if (candidate.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
1468 |         defineSlot(candidate, candidate.getParent(),
1469 |             getNativeType(NO_TYPE), false);
1470 |       }
1471 |     }

===== M0193 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 456-525
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
456 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
457 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
458 |     if (argsParent == null) {
459 |       if (info == null) {
460 |         return this;
461 |       } else {
462 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
463 |       }
464 |     }
465 | 
466 |     // arguments
467 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
468 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
469 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
470 |     }
471 | 
472 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
473 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
474 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
475 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
476 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
477 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
478 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
479 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
480 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
481 | 
482 |       // type from JSDocInfo
483 |       JSType parameterType = null;
484 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
485 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
486 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
487 |         parameterType =
488 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
489 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
490 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
491 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
492 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
493 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
494 |       } else {
495 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
496 |       }
497 | 
498 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
499 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
500 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
501 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
502 |         }
503 |         foundTemplateType = true;
504 |       }
505 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
506 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
507 |           isOptionalParam,
508 |           isVarArgs);
509 | 
510 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
511 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
512 |       }
513 |     }
514 | 
515 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
516 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
517 |     }
518 | 
519 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
520 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
521 |     }
522 | 
523 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
524 |     return this;
525 |   }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

===== M0230 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-262
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
241 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
242 |     guards.add(
243 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
244 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
245 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
246 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
247 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
251 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
252 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
253 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
254 |     // error off.
255 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
256 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
257 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
258 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
260 |     }
261 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
262 |   }

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseObjectLiteral(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 662-696
Documentation:

Method code:
662 |   private FlowScope traverseObjectLiteral(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
663 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
664 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(type);
665 | 
666 |     // Object literals can be reflected on other types, or changed with
667 |     // type casts.
668 |     // See CodingConvention#getObjectLiteralCase and goog.object.reflect.
669 |     // Ignore these types of literals.
670 |     // TODO(nicksantos): There should be an "anonymous object" type that
671 |     // we can check for here.
672 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(type);
673 |     if (objectType == null) {
674 |       return scope;
675 |     }
676 | 
677 |     boolean hasLendsName = n.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
678 |         n.getJSDocInfo().getLendsName() != null;
679 |     if (objectType.hasReferenceName() && !hasLendsName) {
680 |       return scope;
681 |     }
682 | 
683 |     for (Node name = n.getFirstChild(); name != null;
684 |          name = name.getNext()) {
685 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
686 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
687 |       String memberName = NodeUtil.getStringValue(name);
688 |       if (memberName != null) {
689 |         objectType.defineInferredProperty(memberName, getJSType(value), false);
690 |       } else {
691 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
692 |         return scope;
693 |       }
694 |     }
695 |     return scope;
696 |   }

===== M0238 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)

Documentation:

Method code:
630 |     @Override
631 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
632 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
633 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
634 |       }
635 | 
636 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
637 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
638 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
639 |           reportGetter(el);
640 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
641 |           reportSetter(el);
642 |         } else {
643 |           Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
644 |           key.addChildToFront(transform(el.getRight()));
645 |           node.addChildToBack(key);
646 |         }
647 |       }
648 |       return node;
649 |     }

===== M0246 =====

===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 445-791
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
445 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
446 |     JSType childType;
447 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
448 |     Node left, right;
449 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
450 |     boolean typeable = true;
451 | 
452 |     switch (n.getType()) {
453 |       case Token.NAME:
454 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
455 |         break;
456 | 
457 |       case Token.LP:
458 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
459 |         // ignored here.
460 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
461 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
462 |         } else {
463 |           typeable = false;
464 |         }
465 |         break;
466 | 
467 |       case Token.COMMA:
468 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
469 |         break;
470 | 
471 |       case Token.TRUE:
472 |       case Token.FALSE:
473 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
474 |         break;
475 | 
476 |       case Token.THIS:
477 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
478 |         break;
479 | 
480 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
481 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
482 |         break;
483 | 
484 |       case Token.GET_REF:
485 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
486 |         break;
487 | 
488 |       case Token.NULL:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.NUMBER:
493 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
494 |           ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
495 |         } else {
496 |           typeable = false;
497 |         }
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.STRING:
505 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
506 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
507 |         } else {
508 |           typeable = false;
509 |         }
510 |         break;
511 | 
512 |       case Token.REGEXP:
513 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
514 |         break;
515 | 
516 |       case Token.GETPROP:
517 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
518 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
519 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
520 |         break;
521 | 
522 |       case Token.GETELEM:
523 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
524 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
525 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
526 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
527 |         typeable = false;
528 |         break;
529 | 
530 |       case Token.VAR:
531 |         visitVar(t, n);
532 |         typeable = false;
533 |         break;
534 | 
535 |       case Token.NEW:
536 |         visitNew(t, n);
537 |         typeable = true;
538 |         break;
539 | 
540 |       case Token.CALL:
541 |         visitCall(t, n);
542 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
543 |         break;
544 | 
545 |       case Token.RETURN:
546 |         visitReturn(t, n);
547 |         typeable = false;
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.DEC:
551 |       case Token.INC:
552 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
553 |         validator.expectNumber(
554 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
555 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
556 |         break;
557 | 
558 |       case Token.NOT:
559 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
560 |         break;
561 | 
562 |       case Token.VOID:
563 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
567 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
568 |         break;
569 | 
570 |       case Token.BITNOT:
571 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
572 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
573 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
574 |               childType.toString());
575 |         }
576 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
577 |         break;
578 | 
579 |       case Token.POS:
580 |       case Token.NEG:
581 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
582 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
583 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
584 |         break;
585 | 
586 |       case Token.EQ:
587 |       case Token.NE: {
588 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
589 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
590 | 
591 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
592 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
593 |         TernaryValue result =
594 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
595 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
596 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
597 |             result = result.not();
598 |           }
599 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
600 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
601 |         }
602 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
603 |         break;
604 |       }
605 | 
606 |       case Token.SHEQ:
607 |       case Token.SHNE: {
608 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
609 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
610 | 
611 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
612 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
613 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
614 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
615 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
616 |               rightType.toString());
617 |         }
618 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
619 |         break;
620 |       }
621 | 
622 |       case Token.LT:
623 |       case Token.LE:
624 |       case Token.GT:
625 |       case Token.GE:
626 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
627 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
628 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
629 |           validator.expectNumber(
630 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
631 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
632 |           validator.expectNumber(
633 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
634 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
635 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
636 |           // OK.
637 |         } else {
638 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
639 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
640 |           // should match a string context.
641 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
642 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
643 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
644 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
645 |           message = "right side of comparison";
646 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
647 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
648 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
649 |         }
650 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
651 |         break;
652 | 
653 |       case Token.IN:
654 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
655 |         right = n.getLastChild();
656 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
657 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
658 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
659 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
660 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
661 |         break;
662 | 
663 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
664 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
665 |         right = n.getLastChild();
666 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
667 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
668 | 
669 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
670 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
671 |         validator.expectActualObject(
672 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
673 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
674 |         break;
675 | 
676 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
677 |         visitAssign(t, n);
678 |         typeable = false;
679 |         break;
680 | 
681 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
682 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
683 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
684 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
685 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
686 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
688 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
689 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
690 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
691 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
692 |       case Token.LSH:
693 |       case Token.RSH:
694 |       case Token.URSH:
695 |       case Token.DIV:
696 |       case Token.MOD:
697 |       case Token.BITOR:
698 |       case Token.BITXOR:
699 |       case Token.BITAND:
700 |       case Token.SUB:
701 |       case Token.ADD:
702 |       case Token.MUL:
703 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
704 |         break;
705 | 
706 |       case Token.DELPROP:
707 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
708 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
709 |         }
710 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
711 |         break;
712 | 
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
715 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
716 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
717 |         typeable = false;
718 |         break;
719 | 
720 |       case Token.WITH: {
721 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
722 |         childType = getJSType(child);
723 |         validator.expectObject(
724 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
725 |         typeable = false;
726 |         break;
727 |       }
728 | 
729 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
730 |         visitFunction(t, n);
731 |         break;
732 | 
733 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
734 |       case Token.LABEL:
735 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
736 |       case Token.SWITCH:
737 |       case Token.BREAK:
738 |       case Token.CATCH:
739 |       case Token.TRY:
740 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
741 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
742 |       case Token.BLOCK:
743 |       case Token.EMPTY:
744 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
745 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
746 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
747 |       case Token.THROW:
748 |         typeable = false;
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
752 |       case Token.DO:
753 |       case Token.FOR:
754 |       case Token.IF:
755 |       case Token.WHILE:
756 |         typeable = false;
757 |         break;
758 | 
759 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
760 |       case Token.AND:
761 |       case Token.HOOK:
762 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
763 |       case Token.OR:
764 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
765 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
766 |         } else {
767 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
768 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
769 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
770 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
771 |           } else {
772 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
773 |           }
774 |         }
775 |         break;
776 | 
777 |       default:
778 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
779 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
780 |         break;
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
784 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
785 | 
786 |     if (typeable) {
787 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
788 |     }
789 | 
790 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
791 |   }

===== M0261 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 169-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
169 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
170 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
171 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
172 |     // show up in the type registry.
173 |     Scope newScope = null;
174 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
175 |     if (parent == null) {
176 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
177 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
178 | 
179 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
180 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
181 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
182 |     } else {
183 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
184 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
185 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
186 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
187 |     }
188 | 
189 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
190 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
191 | 
192 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
193 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
194 |     // build properties on.
195 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
196 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
197 |       if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
198 |         FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
199 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
200 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
201 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
202 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
203 |         }
204 |       }
205 |     }
206 | 
207 |     if (parent == null) {
208 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
209 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
210 |     }
211 |     return newScope;
212 |   }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 502-552
Documentation:

Method code:
502 |    @Override
503 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
504 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
505 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
506 |       if (name == null) {
507 |         name = new Name();
508 |         name.setIdentifier("");
509 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
510 |       }
511 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
512 |       Node newName = transform(name);
513 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
514 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
515 |         // declaration.
516 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
517 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
518 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
519 |         // same line as the function keyword.
520 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
521 |             functionNode.getLp();
522 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
523 |       }
524 | 
525 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
526 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
527 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
528 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
529 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
530 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
531 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
532 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
533 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
534 |       if (fnName != null) {
535 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
536 |       } else {
537 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
538 |       }
539 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
540 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
541 | 
542 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
543 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
544 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
545 |       }
546 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
547 | 
548 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
549 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
550 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
551 |      return node;
552 |     }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 149-175

/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
149 |   JSType build() {
150 |     if (result == null) {
151 |       if (isAllType) {
152 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
153 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
154 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {

162 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
163 |         } else {
164 |           if (size > 1) {

166 |           } else if (size == 1) {
167 |             result = alternates.iterator().next();
168 |           } else {

170 |           }
171 |         }
172 |       }
173 |     }

===== M0299 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 343-387
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
343 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
344 |     if (info != null) {
345 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
346 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
347 | 
348 |       // base type
349 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
350 |         if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
351 |           JSType maybeBaseType =
352 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
353 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
354 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
355 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
356 |           }
357 |         } else {
358 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
359 |         }
360 |       }
361 | 
362 |       // implemented interfaces
363 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
364 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
365 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
366 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
367 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
368 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
369 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
370 |           }
371 |         }
372 |         if (baseType != null) {
373 |           JSType maybeFunctionType = baseType.getConstructor();
374 |           if (maybeFunctionType instanceof FunctionType) {
375 |             FunctionType functionType = baseType.getConstructor();
376 |             Iterables.addAll(
377 |                 implementedInterfaces,
378 |                 functionType.getImplementedInterfaces());
379 |           }
380 |         }
381 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
382 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
383 |       }
384 |     }
385 | 
386 |     return this;
387 |   }

   * should be either a subtype or supertype of the second.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point.
   * @param type The type being cast from.
   * @param castType The type being cast to.
   */
Method code:
438 |   void expectCanCast(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, JSType castType) {
439 |     castType = castType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
440 |     type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
441 | 
442 |     if (!type.canAssignTo(castType) && !castType.canAssignTo(type)) {
443 |       if (shouldReport) {
444 |         compiler.report(
445 |             t.makeError(n, INVALID_CAST,
446 |                 castType.toString(), type.toString()));
447 |       }
448 |       registerMismatch(type, castType);
449 |     }
450 |   }
