===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 408-452
Documentation:

Method code:
408 |   @Override
409 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
410 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
411 |       return true;
412 |     }
413 | 
414 |     // Union types
415 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
416 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
417 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
418 |       return false;
419 |     }
420 | 
421 |     // record types
422 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
423 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Interfaces
427 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
428 |     // to the interface instance.
429 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
430 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
431 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
432 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
433 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
434 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
435 |           return true;
436 |         }
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // other prototype based objects
441 |     if (that != null) {
442 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
443 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
444 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
445 |         // to avoid guessing.
446 |         return true;
447 |       }
448 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
449 |     }
450 | 
451 |     return false;
452 |   }

===== M0311 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 477-516
Documentation:

Method code:
477 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
478 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
479 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
480 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
481 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
482 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
483 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
484 |       return;
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
488 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
489 | 
490 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
491 |     // function (unless it is called)
492 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
493 |       child = child.getNext();
494 |     }
495 | 
496 |     if (child != null) {
497 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
498 |     } else {
499 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
500 |     }
501 | 
502 |     // Synthetic blocks
503 |     if (parent != null) {
504 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
505 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
506 |         case Token.CASE:
507 |         case Token.TRY:
508 |           break;
509 |         default:
510 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
511 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
512 |           }
513 |           break;
514 |       }
515 |     }
516 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1361-1391
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1361 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1362 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1363 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1364 | 
1365 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1366 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1367 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1368 |       return;
1369 |     }
1370 | 
1371 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1372 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1373 |     if (childType instanceof FunctionType) {
1374 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) childType;
1375 | 
1376 |       // Non-native constructors should never be called directly.
1377 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1378 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType()) {
1379 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1380 |       }
1381 | 
1382 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1383 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1384 |     } else {
1385 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1386 |     }
1387 | 
1388 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1389 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1390 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1391 |   }

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 69-110
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
69 |   @Override
70 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
71 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
72 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
73 |     if (type != null) {
74 |       Node subclass = null;
75 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
76 | 
77 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
78 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
79 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
80 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
81 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
82 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
83 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
84 |       if (callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
85 |           callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
86 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
87 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
88 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
89 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
90 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
94 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
95 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
96 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
97 |       if (subclass != null &&
98 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
99 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
100 |         // make sure to strip the prototype off of the nodes
101 |         // to normalize for goog.mixin
102 |         return new SubclassRelationship(
103 |             type,
104 |             stripPrototype(subclass),
105 |             stripPrototype(superclass));
106 |       }
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     return null;
110 |   }

===== M0329 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 577-607
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
577 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
578 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
579 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
580 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
581 |     if (isOptional) {
582 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
583 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
584 |       // error can be reported.
585 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
586 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
587 |         emittedWarning = true;
588 |       }
589 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
590 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
591 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
592 |         emittedWarning = true;
593 |       }
594 |     } else {
595 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
596 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
597 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
598 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
599 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
600 |         } else {
601 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
602 |         }
603 |         emittedWarning = true;
604 |       }
605 |     }
606 |     return emittedWarning;
607 |   }

===== M0330 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 82-178
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0336 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1434-1436
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1434 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1435 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1436 |   }

   * @param jsTypeName The name string.

   *     otherwise the corresponding JSType object.

===== M0347 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 183-199
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
183 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
184 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
185 | 
186 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
187 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
188 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
189 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
190 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
191 |       }
192 | 
193 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
194 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
195 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
196 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
197 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
198 |       }
199 |     }

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 407-416
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
407 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
408 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
409 |          current != null;
410 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
411 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
412 |         return true;
413 |       }
414 |     }
415 |     return false;
416 |   }

===== M0357 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.detectImplicitPrototypeCycle()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 135-155
Documentation:
/**
   * Detects a cycle in the implicit prototype chain. This method accesses
   * the {@link #getImplicitPrototype()} method and must therefore be
   * invoked only after the object is sufficiently initialized to respond to
   * calls to this method.<p>
   *
   * The method is not thread safe.<p>
   *
   * @return True iff an implicit prototype cycle was detected.
   */
Method code:
135 |   final boolean detectImplicitPrototypeCycle() {
136 |     // detecting cycle
137 |     this.visited = true;
138 |     ObjectType p = getImplicitPrototype();
139 |     while (p != null) {
140 |       if (p.visited) {
141 |         return true;
142 |       } else {
143 |         p.visited = true;
144 |       }
145 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
146 |     }
147 | 
148 |     // clean up
149 |     p = this;
150 |     do {
151 |       p.visited = false;
152 |       p = p.getImplicitPrototype();
153 |     } while (p != null);
154 |     return false;
155 |   }

===== M0359 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1455-1490
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1455 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1456 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1457 | 
1458 |     skipEOLs();
1459 | 
1460 |     boolean restArg = false;
1461 |     token = next();
1462 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1463 |       token = next();
1464 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1465 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1466 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1467 |       }
1468 |       restArg = true;
1469 |     }
1470 | 
1471 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1472 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1473 |       skipEOLs();
1474 |       if (restArg) {
1475 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1476 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1477 |         next();
1478 |         skipEOLs();
1479 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1480 |       }
1481 | 
1482 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1483 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1484 |       } else {
1485 |         next();
1486 |       }
1487 |     }
1488 | 
1489 |     return typeNode;
1490 |   }

===== M0383 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

===== M0387 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1097-1126
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1097 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1098 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1099 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1100 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1101 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1102 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1103 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1104 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1105 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1106 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1107 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1108 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1109 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1110 |       return false;
1111 |     }
1112 | 
1113 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1114 |     if (type == null) {
1115 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1116 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1117 |       if (var != null) {
1118 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1119 |         if (varType != null) {
1120 |           type = varType;
1121 |         }
1122 |       }
1123 |     }
1124 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1125 |     return true;
1126 |   }

Method code:
56 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
57 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {

59 |       addGuards(Iterables.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));

===== M0392 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineName(Node,Node,Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java

Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a variable based on the {@link Token#NAME} node passed.
     * @param name The {@link Token#NAME} node.
     * @param var The parent of the {@code name} node, which must be a
     *     {@link Token#VAR} node.
     * @param parent {@code var}'s parent.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} information relating to this
     *     {@code name} node.
     */
Method code:
698 |     private void defineName(Node name, Node var, Node parent, JSDocInfo info) {
699 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
700 | 
701 |       if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
702 |         // function
703 |         String functionName = name.getString();
704 |         FunctionType functionType =
705 |             getFunctionType(functionName, value, info, null);
706 |         if (functionType.isReturnTypeInferred() &&
707 |             scope.isLocal()) {
708 |           defineSlot(name, var, null);
709 |         } else {
710 |           defineSlot(name, var, functionType);
711 |         }
712 |       } else {
713 |         // variable's type
714 |         JSType type = null;
715 |         if (info == null) {
716 |           // the variable's type will be inferred
717 |           CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
718 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(input, sourceName);
719 |           type = input.isExtern() ?
720 |               getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : null;
721 |         } else if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
722 |           type = getEnumType(name.getString(), var, value,
723 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
724 |         } else if (info.isConstructor()) {
725 |           type = getFunctionType(name.getString(), value, info, name);
726 |         } else {
727 |           type = getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, name, info);
728 |         }
729 | 
730 |         defineSlot(name, var, type);
731 |       }
732 |     }

===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.registerMismatch(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 570-593
Documentation:

Method code:
570 |   private void registerMismatch(JSType found, JSType required) {
571 |     // Don't register a mismatch for differences in null or undefined or if the
572 |     // code didn't downcast.
573 |     found = found.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
574 |     required = required.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
575 |     if (found.canAssignTo(required) || required.canAssignTo(found)) {
576 |       return;
577 |     }
578 | 
579 |     mismatches.add(new TypeMismatch(found, required));
580 |     if (found instanceof FunctionType &&
581 |         required instanceof FunctionType) {
582 |       FunctionType fnTypeA = ((FunctionType) found);
583 |       FunctionType fnTypeB = ((FunctionType) required);
584 |       Iterator<Node> paramItA = fnTypeA.getParameters().iterator();
585 |       Iterator<Node> paramItB = fnTypeB.getParameters().iterator();
586 |       while (paramItA.hasNext() && paramItB.hasNext()) {
587 |         registerIfMismatch(paramItA.next().getJSType(),
588 |             paramItB.next().getJSType());
589 |       }
590 | 
591 |       registerIfMismatch(fnTypeA.getReturnType(), fnTypeB.getReturnType());
592 |     }
593 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1291-1321
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1291 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1292 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1293 |         Node n = stub.node;
1294 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1295 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1296 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1297 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1298 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1299 | 
1300 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1301 |           continue;
1302 |         }
1303 | 
1304 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1305 |         // in the type registry.
1306 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1307 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1308 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1309 | 
1310 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1311 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1312 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1313 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1314 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1315 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern);
1316 |         } else {
1317 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1318 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1319 |         }
1320 |       }
1321 |     }

===== M0402 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 187-286
Documentation:

Method code:
187 |   @Override
188 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
189 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
190 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
191 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
192 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
193 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
194 |     // worthwhile.
195 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
196 |     Node condition = null;
197 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
198 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
199 | 
200 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
201 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
202 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
203 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
204 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
205 | 
206 |       switch (branch) {
207 |         case ON_TRUE:
208 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
209 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
210 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
211 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
212 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
213 |             }
214 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
215 |               FlowScope informed = output.createChildFlowScope();
216 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(),
217 |                   getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
218 |               newScope = informed;
219 |             }
220 |             break;
221 |           }
222 | 
223 |           // FALL THROUGH
224 | 
225 |         case ON_FALSE:
226 |           if (condition == null) {
227 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
228 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
229 |               condition = source;
230 | 
231 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
232 |               // of the loop.
233 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
234 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
235 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
236 |               }
237 |             }
238 |           }
239 | 
240 |           if (condition != null) {
241 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
242 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
243 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
244 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
245 |               // scope on false.
246 |               //
247 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
248 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
249 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
250 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
251 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
252 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
253 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
254 | 
255 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
256 |               // of the loop.
257 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
258 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
259 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
260 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
261 |               }
262 |               newScope =
263 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
264 |                       condition,
265 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
266 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
267 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
268 |             } else {
269 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
270 |               // of the loop.
271 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
272 |                 conditionFlowScope =
273 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
274 |               }
275 |               newScope =
276 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
277 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
278 |             }
279 |           }
280 |           break;
281 |       }
282 | 
283 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
284 |     }
285 |     return result;
286 |   }

===== M0417 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 84-106
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

===== M0421 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(Node,ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 593-612
Documentation:
/**
   * Declares a property on its owner, if necessary.
   * @return True if a property was declared.
   */
Method code:
593 |   private boolean ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(
594 |       Node getprop, ObjectType objectType) {
595 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
596 |     String qName = getprop.getQualifiedName();
597 |     if (qName != null) {
598 |       Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(qName);
599 |       if (var != null && !var.isTypeInferred()) {
600 |         // Handle normal declarations that could not be addressed earlier.
601 |         if (propName.equals("prototype") ||
602 |         // Handle prototype declarations that could not be addressed earlier.
603 |             (!objectType.hasOwnProperty(propName) &&
604 |              (!objectType.isInstanceType() ||
605 |                  (var.isExtern() && !objectType.isNativeObjectType())))) {
606 |           return objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
607 |               propName, var.getType(), var.isExtern());
608 |         }
609 |       }
610 |     }
611 |     return false;
612 |   }

380 |   private void checkNoTypeCheckSection(Node n, boolean enterSection) {
381 |     switch (n.getType()) {
382 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
383 |       case Token.BLOCK:
384 |       case Token.VAR:
385 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
386 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
387 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
388 |         if (info != null && info.isNoTypeCheck()) {
389 |           if (enterSection) {
390 |             noTypeCheckSection++;
391 |           } else {
392 |             noTypeCheckSection--;
393 |           }
394 |         }
395 |         validator.setShouldReport(noTypeCheckSection == 0);
396 |         break;
397 |     }
398 |   }

===== M0429 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 120-170
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
121 |       // null is the smallest value
122 |       if (p2 == null) {
123 |         if (p1 == null) {
124 |           return 0;
125 |         } else {
126 |           return P1_GT_P2;
127 |         }
128 |       }
129 | 
130 |       // check level
131 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
132 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
133 |       }
134 | 
135 |       // sourceName comparison
136 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
137 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
138 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
139 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
140 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
141 |           return sourceCompare;
142 |         }
143 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
144 |         return P1_LT_P2;
145 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
146 |         return P1_GT_P2;
147 |       }
148 |       // lineno comparison
149 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
150 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
151 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
152 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
153 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
154 |         return P1_LT_P2;
155 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
156 |         return P1_GT_P2;
157 |       }
158 |       // charno comparison
159 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
160 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
161 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
162 |         return charno1 - charno2;
163 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
164 |         return P1_LT_P2;
165 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
166 |         return P1_GT_P2;
167 |       }
168 |       // description
169 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
170 |     }

===== M0430 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(String,JSDocInfo,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 245-264
Documentation:

Method code:
245 |   @Override
246 |   public void setPropertyJSDocInfo(String propertyName, JSDocInfo info,
247 |       boolean inExterns) {
248 |     if (info != null) {
249 |       if (!properties.containsKey(propertyName)) {
250 |         // If docInfo was attached, but the type of the property
251 |         // was not defined anywhere, then we consider this an explicit
252 |         // declaration of the property.
253 |         defineInferredProperty(propertyName, getPropertyType(propertyName),
254 |             inExterns);
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       // The prototype property is not represented as a normal Property.
258 |       // We probably don't want to attach any JSDoc to it anyway.
259 |       Property property = properties.get(propertyName);
260 |       if (property != null) {
261 |         property.docInfo = info;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1569 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1570 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1571 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1572 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1573 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1574 |       // {?} - right curly
1575 |       // {?=} - equals
1576 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1577 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1578 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1579 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1580 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1581 |       token = next();
1582 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1583 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1584 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1585 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1586 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1587 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1588 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1589 |       }
1590 | 
1591 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1592 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1593 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1594 |     } else {
1595 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1596 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1597 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1598 |           next();
1599 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1600 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1601 |           next();
1602 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1603 |         }
1604 |       }
1605 | 
1606 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1607 |     }
1608 |   }

===== M0435 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)

Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
294 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
295 |       Node ret = transform(n);
296 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
297 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
298 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
299 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
300 |       }
301 |       return ret;
302 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)

Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.

Method code:
774 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
775 |     switch (n.getType()) {
776 |       case Token.DO:
777 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());

783 |       case Token.LABEL:

785 |       default:
786 |         return n;
787 |     }

===== M0457 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 614-653
Documentation:

Method code:
614 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
615 |     String varName = n.getString();
616 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
617 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
618 |     if (value != null) {
619 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
620 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
621 |           getJSType(value));
622 |       return scope;
623 |     } else {
624 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
625 |       if (var != null) {
626 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
627 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
628 | 
629 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
630 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
631 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
632 |         boolean unflowable =
633 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
634 | 
635 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
636 |         // inferred variable.
637 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
638 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
639 |             isInferred &&
640 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
641 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
642 | 
643 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
644 |           type = var.getType();
645 |           if (type == null) {
646 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
647 |           }
648 |         }
649 |       }
650 |     }
651 |     n.setJSType(type);
652 |     return scope;
653 |   }

===== M0467 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1246-1279
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1246 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1247 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1248 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1249 |     // merge to fix this.
1250 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1251 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1252 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1253 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1254 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1255 | 
1256 |       if (value != null) {
1257 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1258 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1259 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1260 | 
1261 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1262 |         if (info == null) {
1263 |           info = varInfo;
1264 |         }
1265 |         if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1266 |           // var.getType() can never be null, this would indicate a bug in the
1267 |           // scope creation logic.
1268 |           checkEnumInitializer(
1269 |               t, value,
1270 |               info.getEnumParameterType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry));
1271 |         } else if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1272 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1273 |         } else {
1274 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1275 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1276 |         }
1277 |       }
1278 |     }
1279 |   }

===== M0468 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 148-193
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
148 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
149 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
150 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
151 |       case Token.BLOCK:
152 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
153 |       case Token.TRY:
154 |       case Token.FINALLY:
155 |         return true;
156 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
157 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
158 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
159 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
160 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
161 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
162 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
163 |         // child.
164 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
165 |       case Token.WHILE:
166 |       case Token.DO:
167 |       case Token.IF:
168 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
169 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
170 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
171 | 
172 |       case Token.FOR:
173 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
174 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
175 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
176 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
177 |         // That way the following:
178 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
179 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
180 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
181 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
182 |         } else {
183 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
184 |         }
185 |       case Token.SWITCH:
186 |       case Token.CASE:
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |       case Token.WITH:
189 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
190 |       default:
191 |         return false;
192 |     }
193 |   }

826 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {

===== M0492 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1336-1485
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1336 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1337 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope, boolean forgiving) {
1338 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1339 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1340 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1341 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1342 | 
1343 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1344 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1345 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, forgiving)
1346 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1347 | 
1348 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1349 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1350 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1351 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1352 |         }
1353 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1354 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1355 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope, forgiving));
1356 | 
1357 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1358 |         return createOptionalType(
1359 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1360 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1361 | 
1362 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1363 |         return createOptionalType(
1364 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1365 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1366 | 
1367 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1368 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1369 | 
1370 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1371 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1372 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1373 | 
1374 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1375 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1376 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1377 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1378 |           builder.addAlternate(
1379 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope, false));
1380 |         }
1381 |         return builder.build();
1382 | 
1383 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1384 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1385 | 
1386 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1387 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1388 | 
1389 |       case Token.STRING:
1390 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1391 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1392 |         if (forgiving) {
1393 |           namedType.forgiveUnknownNames();
1394 |         }
1395 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1396 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1397 |         }
1398 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1399 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1400 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1401 |           if (typeList != null &&
1402 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1403 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1404 |             JSType parameterType =
1405 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1406 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1407 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1408 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1409 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1410 |               JSType indexType =
1411 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1412 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1413 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1414 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1415 |             }
1416 |           }
1417 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1418 |         } else {
1419 |           return namedType;
1420 |         }
1421 | 
1422 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1423 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1424 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1425 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS) {
1426 |           Node thisNode = current.getFirstChild();
1427 |           thisType =
1428 |               ObjectType.cast(
1429 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1430 |                       thisNode, sourceName, scope, false)
1431 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1432 |           if (thisType == null) {
1433 |             reporter.warning(
1434 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject"),
1435 |                 sourceName, thisNode.getLineno(), "", thisNode.getCharno());
1436 |           }
1437 | 
1438 |           current = current.getNext();
1439 |         }
1440 | 
1441 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1442 | 
1443 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1444 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1445 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1446 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1447 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1448 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1449 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1450 |               } else {
1451 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1452 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1453 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1454 |               }
1455 |             } else {
1456 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1457 |                   arg, sourceName, scope, false);
1458 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1459 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1460 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1461 |                   reporter.warning(
1462 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1463 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1464 |                 }
1465 |               } else {
1466 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1467 |               }
1468 |             }
1469 |           }
1470 |           current = current.getNext();
1471 |         }
1472 | 
1473 |         JSType returnType =
1474 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope, false);
1475 | 
1476 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1477 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1478 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1479 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1480 |             .build();
1481 |     }
1482 | 
1483 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1484 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1485 |   }

Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.

Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {

313 |     } else {

===== M0510 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M0511 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 841-863
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
841 |   private void updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
842 |     int i = 0;
843 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
844 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
845 |       JSType iParameterType = iParameter.getJSType();
846 |       if (iParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
847 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = (FunctionType) iParameterType;
848 | 
849 |         if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
850 |           // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
851 |           return;
852 |         }
853 |         Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
854 |         JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
855 |         if (iArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
856 |             iArgumentType instanceof FunctionType &&
857 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
858 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
859 |         }
860 |       }
861 |       i++;
862 |     }
863 |   }

118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {

136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:

140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {
270 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.

   * @see #analyze()

125 |     if (nodeComparator != null) {
126 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);
127 |     } else {
128 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
129 |     }
130 |   }

===== M0528 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1030-1064
Documentation:

Method code:
1030 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1031 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1032 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1033 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1034 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1035 |     if (var != null) {
1036 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1037 |       if (varType != null) {
1038 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1039 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1040 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1041 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1042 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1043 |         } else {
1044 |           return varType;
1045 |         }
1046 |       }
1047 |     }
1048 | 
1049 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1050 |     if (objType != null) {
1051 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1052 |     }
1053 | 
1054 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1055 |         qualifiedName != null) {
1056 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1057 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1058 |       if (regType != null) {
1059 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1060 |       }
1061 |     }
1062 | 
1063 |     return propertyType;
1064 |   }

621 |       if (n.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
622 |         if (info != null) {
623 |           // multiple children
624 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, MULTIPLE_VAR_DEF));
625 |         }
626 |         for (Node name : n.children()) {
627 |           defineName(name, n, parent, name.getJSDocInfo());
628 |         }
629 |       } else {
630 |         Node name = n.getFirstChild();
631 |         defineName(name, n, parent,
632 |             (info != null) ? info : name.getJSDocInfo());
633 |       }
634 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:

1044 |         true);

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1396-1430
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1396 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1397 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1398 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1399 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1400 | 
1401 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1402 |     int ordinal = 0;
1403 |     Node parameter = null;
1404 |     Node argument = null;
1405 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1406 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1407 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1408 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1409 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1410 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1411 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1412 |       }
1413 |       argument = arguments.next();
1414 |       ordinal++;
1415 | 
1416 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1417 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1418 |     }
1419 | 
1420 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1421 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1422 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1423 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1424 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1425 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1426 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1427 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1428 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1429 |     }
1430 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSDocString(int)

Original range: 280-304
Documentation:

Method code:
280 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
281 |     switch (c) {
282 |       case '@':
283 |       case '*':
284 |       case ',':
285 |       case '>':
286 |       case ':':
287 |       case '(':
288 |       case ')':
289 |       case '{':
290 |       case '}':
291 |       case '[':
292 |       case ']':
293 |       case '?':
294 |       case '!':
295 |       case '|':

297 |       case EOF_CHAR:

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 210-223
Documentation:
/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
210 |   public int getMinArguments() {
211 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
212 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
213 |     // of the last required parameter.
214 |     int i = 0;
215 |     int min = 0;
216 |     for (Node n : getParameters()) {
217 |       i++;
218 |       if (!n.isOptionalArg() && !n.isVarArgs()) {
219 |         min = i;
220 |       }
221 |     }
222 |     return min;
223 |   }
