===== M0596 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 671-707
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
671 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
672 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
673 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
674 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
675 | 
676 |     if (existingType != null) {
677 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
678 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
679 |         FunctionType existingFn =
680 |             isInstanceObject ?
681 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
682 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
683 | 
684 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
685 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
686 |         }
687 | 
688 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
689 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
690 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
691 |         }
692 | 
693 |         return existingFn;
694 |       } else {
695 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
696 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
697 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
698 |       }
699 |     }
700 | 
701 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
702 | 
703 |     if (scope.isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
704 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
705 |     }
706 |     return fnType;
707 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)

Original range: 1646-1668
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1646 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1647 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1648 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1649 |     }
1650 | 
1651 |     Node typeName = newStringNode(stream.getString());
1652 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1653 |       next();
1654 |       skipEOLs();
1655 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1656 |       if (memberType != null) {
1657 |         typeName.addChildToFront(memberType);
1658 | 
1659 |         skipEOLs();
1660 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1661 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1662 |         }
1663 | 
1664 |         next();
1665 |       }
1666 |     }
1667 |     return typeName;
1668 |   }

/**

   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1726 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1727 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1728 |       return getString();
1729 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1730 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1731 |       if (left == null) {
1732 |         return null;
1733 |       }
1734 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1735 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1736 |       return "this";
1737 |     } else {
1738 |       return null;
1739 |     }
1740 |   }

===== M0611 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 87-142
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
87 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
88 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
89 |     // just bail out early here.
90 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
91 |       return this;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
95 | 
96 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
97 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
98 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
99 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
100 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
101 |     }
102 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
103 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
104 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
105 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
106 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
107 |         }
108 |       } else {
109 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
110 |           return this;
111 |         }
112 | 
113 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
114 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
115 |         // one another.
116 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
117 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
118 |           JSType current = it.next();
119 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
120 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
121 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
122 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
123 |               return this;
124 |             }
125 |           } else {
126 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
127 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
128 |               return this;
129 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
130 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
131 |               it.remove();
132 |             }
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |         alternates.add(alternate);
136 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
137 |       }
138 |     } else {
139 |       result = null;
140 |     }
141 |     return this;
142 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.getReadableJSTypeName(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 621-664
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a node, get a human-readable name for the type of that node so
   * that will be easy for the programmer to find the original declaration.
   *
   * For example, if SubFoo's property "bar" might have the human-readable
   * name "Foo.prototype.bar".
   *
   * @param n The node.
   * @param dereference If true, the type of the node will be dereferenced
   *     to an Object type, if possible.
   */
Method code:
621 |   String getReadableJSTypeName(Node n, boolean dereference) {
622 |     // If we're analyzing a GETPROP, the property may be inherited by the
623 |     // prototype chain. So climb the prototype chain and find out where
624 |     // the property was originally defined.
625 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
626 |       ObjectType objectType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild()).dereference();
627 |       if (objectType != null) {
628 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
629 |         while (objectType != null && !objectType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
630 |           objectType = objectType.getImplicitPrototype();
631 |         }
632 | 
633 |         // Don't show complex function names or anonymous types.
634 |         // Instead, try to get a human-readable type name.
635 |         if (objectType != null &&
636 |             (objectType.getConstructor() != null ||
637 |              objectType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
638 |           return objectType.toString() + "." + propName;
639 |         }
640 |       }
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     JSType type = getJSType(n);
644 |     if (dereference) {
645 |       ObjectType dereferenced = type.dereference();
646 |       if (dereferenced != null) {
647 |         type = dereferenced;
648 |       }
649 |     }
650 | 
651 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
652 |     if (type.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
653 |         (type.toObjectType() != null &&
654 |          type.toObjectType().getConstructor() != null)) {
655 |       return type.toString();
656 |     } else if (qualifiedName != null) {
657 |       return qualifiedName;
658 |     } else if (type instanceof FunctionType) {
659 |       // Don't show complex function names.
660 |       return "function";
661 |     } else {
662 |       return type.toString();
663 |     }
664 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1614-1639
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1614 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1615 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1616 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1617 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1618 |       skipEOLs();
1619 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1620 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1621 |       skipEOLs();
1622 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1623 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1624 |       skipEOLs();
1625 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1626 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1627 |       String string = stream.getString();
1628 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1629 |         skipEOLs();
1630 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1631 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1632 |         return newStringNode(string);
1633 |       } else {
1634 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1635 |       }
1636 |     }
1637 | 
1638 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1639 |   }

204 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
205 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
206 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
207 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
208 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
212 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
213 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
214 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
215 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
216 |     } else {

218 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
219 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
220 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
221 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();

223 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 653-679
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
653 |   @Override
654 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
655 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
656 |       return false;
657 |     }
658 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
659 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
660 |       return false;
661 |     }
662 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
663 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
664 |         return this == that;
665 |       }
666 |       return false;
667 |     }
668 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
669 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
670 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
671 |       }
672 |       return false;
673 |     }
674 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
675 |       return false;
676 |     }
677 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
678 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
679 |   }

/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.

   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.

Method code:
1136 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1137 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1138 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1139 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1140 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1141 |       return;

1153 |     // non-object.

1155 |             "undefined has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1156 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1157 |       return;
1158 |     }
1159 | 
1160 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1161 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1162 |   }

Original range: 1315-1352
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1315 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1316 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1317 | 
1318 |     FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
1319 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1320 |     if (functionType.isInterface() || functionType.isConstructor()) {
1321 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.
1322 |           getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype().getConstructor();
1323 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1324 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1325 |           (baseConstructor.isConstructor() && functionType.isInterface() ||
1326 |            baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1327 |         compiler.report(
1328 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1329 |       }
1330 | 
1331 |       for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1332 |         boolean badImplementedType = false;
1333 |         ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1334 |         if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1335 |           FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1336 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1337 |           if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1338 |               !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1339 |             badImplementedType = true;
1340 |           }
1341 |         } else {
1342 |           badImplementedType = true;
1343 |         }
1344 |         if (badImplementedType) {
1345 |           report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1346 |         }
1347 |       }
1348 |       if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1349 |         validator.expectAllInterfacePropertiesImplemented(functionType);
1350 |       }
1351 |     }
1352 |   }

===== M0680 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(String,Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 550-595
Documentation:

Method code:
550 |     JSType getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(String sourceName,
551 |         Node node, JSDocInfo info) {
552 |       JSType jsType = null;
553 |       Node objNode = node.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
554 |           node.getFirstChild() : null;
555 |       if (info != null) {
556 |         if (info.hasType()) {
557 |           jsType = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
558 |         } else if (FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info)) {
559 |           String fnName = node.getQualifiedName();
560 | 
561 |           // constructors are often handled separately.
562 |           if (info.isConstructor() && typeRegistry.getType(fnName) != null) {
563 |             return null;
564 |           }
565 | 
566 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
567 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(
568 |                   fnName, compiler, node, sourceName, scope)
569 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
570 |               .inferReturnType(info)
571 |               .inferParameterTypes(info)
572 |               .inferInheritance(info);
573 | 
574 |           // Infer the context type.
575 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
576 |           if (objNode != null) {
577 |             if (objNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
578 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
579 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getFirstChild());
580 |               searchedForThisType = true;
581 |             } else if (objNode.getType() == Token.THIS) {
582 |               builder.inferThisType(info, objNode.getJSType());
583 |               searchedForThisType = true;
584 |             }
585 |           }
586 | 
587 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
588 |             builder.inferThisType(info, (Node) null);
589 |           }
590 | 
591 |           jsType = builder.buildAndRegister();
592 |         }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)

Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0692 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 791-835
Documentation:

Method code:
791 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
792 |       Node callNode) {
793 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
794 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
795 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
796 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
797 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
798 |       return scope;
799 |     }
800 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
801 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
802 |       return scope;
803 |     }
804 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
805 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
806 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
807 |     if (assertedType == null) {
808 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
809 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
810 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
811 |         if (type != narrowed) {
812 |           scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
813 |           redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
814 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
815 |         }
816 |       } else if (assertedNode.getType() == Token.AND ||
817 |                  assertedNode.getType() == Token.OR) {
818 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
819 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
820 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
821 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
822 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
823 |       }
824 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
825 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
826 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
827 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
828 |       if (type != narrowed) {
829 |         scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
830 |         redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
831 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
832 |       }
833 |     }
834 |     return scope;
835 |   }

   * Two named types are equivalent if they are the same {@code
   * ObjectType} object.  This is complicated by the fact that isEquivalent
   * is sometimes called before we have a chance to resolve the type
   * names.
   *
   * @return {@code true} iff {@code that} == {@code this} or {@code that}

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 250-293
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.

===== M0722 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 144-185
Documentation:

Method code:
144 |   @Override
145 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
146 |     this.root = root;
147 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
148 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
149 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
150 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities);
151 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
152 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
153 | 
154 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
155 |     // search on the CFG.
156 |     priorityCounter = 0;
157 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
158 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
159 | 
160 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
161 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
162 |       // priority of them too.
163 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
164 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
165 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
166 |           Preconditions.checkState(
167 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
168 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
169 |         }
170 |       }
171 |     }
172 | 
173 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
174 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
175 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
176 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
177 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
178 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
179 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
180 |       }
181 |     }
182 | 
183 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
184 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
185 |   }

===== M0731 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 232-283
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
232 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
233 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
234 |     if (oldType == null) {
235 |       return this;
236 |     }
237 | 
238 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
239 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
240 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
241 |       // Not a function literal.
242 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
243 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
244 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
245 |       }
246 |     } else {
247 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
248 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
249 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
250 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
251 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
252 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
253 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
254 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
255 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
256 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
257 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
258 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
259 | 
260 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
261 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
262 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
263 | 
264 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
265 |           // arguments.
266 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
267 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
268 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
269 |           }
270 |         } else {
271 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
272 |               paramBuilder,
273 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
274 |               warnedAboutArgList,
275 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
276 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
277 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
278 |         }
279 |       }
280 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
281 |     }
282 |     return this;
283 |   }

===== M0738 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 407-433
Documentation:

Method code:
407 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
408 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
409 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
410 |     switch (n.getType()) {
411 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
412 |         // normal type checking
413 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
414 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
415 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
416 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
417 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
418 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
419 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
420 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
421 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
422 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
423 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
424 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
425 |         }
426 | 
427 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
428 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
429 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
430 |         break;
431 |     }
432 |     return true;
433 |   }

===== M0750 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 463-518
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
463 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, Node n, Node parent, Var var,
464 |       String variableName, JSType newType) {
465 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
466 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
467 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
468 |       if (info == null) {
469 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
470 |       }
471 |       allowDupe =
472 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
476 | 
477 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
478 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
479 |     // compilation process.
480 |     if (varType != null &&
481 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
482 |         newType != null &&
483 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
484 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
485 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
486 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
487 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
488 |       // native type.
489 |       if (var.input == null) {
490 |         n.setJSType(varType);
491 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
492 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
493 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
494 |           }
495 |         } else {
496 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
497 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
498 |         }
499 |       } else {
500 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
501 |         // match the original type.
502 |         //
503 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
504 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
505 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
506 |               var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) ||
507 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
508 |           if (shouldReport) {
509 |             compiler.report(
510 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
511 |                     variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
512 |                     String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
513 |                     varType.toString()));
514 |           }
515 |         }
516 |       }
517 |     }
518 |   }

874 |     // activation object. In ES5, it leaves it as null. Just punt on this
875 |     // issue for now by coercing out null. This is complicated by the

889 |       resolvedInterfaces.add(resolvedIface);
890 |       changed |= (resolvedIface != iface);
891 |     }
892 |     if (changed) {
893 |       implementedInterfaces = resolvedInterfaces.build();
894 |     }
895 |

===== M0761 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 930-1010
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a typed variable. The defining node will be annotated with the
     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is
     * inferred.
     *
     * Slots may be any variable or any qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     */
Method code:
930 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, JSType type, boolean inferred) {
931 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(inferred || type != null);
932 | 
933 |       // Only allow declarations of NAMEs and qualfied names.
934 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = false;
935 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
936 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
937 |             parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
938 |             parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
939 |             parent.getType() == Token.LP ||
940 |             parent.getType() == Token.CATCH);
941 |         shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = scope.isGlobal() &&
942 |             (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
943 |              parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
944 |       } else {
945 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
946 |             n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
947 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN ||
948 |              parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT));
949 |       }
950 |       String variableName = n.getQualifiedName();
951 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
952 | 
953 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
954 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
955 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
956 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
957 |       if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP && !scope.isGlobal() &&
958 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
959 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
960 | 
961 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
962 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
963 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
964 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
965 |         }
966 |       }
967 | 
968 |       // declared in closest scope?
969 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
970 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
971 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
972 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
973 |       } else {
974 |         if (!inferred) {
975 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
976 |         }
977 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
978 |         scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
979 | 
980 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
981 |           ObjectType globalThis =
982 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
983 |           boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
984 |           if (inferred) {
985 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
986 |                 type == null ?
987 |                     getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
988 |                     type,
989 |                 isExtern);
990 |           } else {
991 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern);
992 |           }
993 |         }
994 | 
995 |         // If we're in the global scope, also declare var.prototype
996 |         // in the scope chain.
997 |         if (scopeToDeclareIn.isGlobal() && type instanceof FunctionType) {
998 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
999 |           if (fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) {
1000 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1001 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
1002 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), compiler.getInput(sourceName),
1003 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1004 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
1005 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1006 |                     getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1007 |           }
1008 |         }
1009 |       }
1010 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 191-210
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
191 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
192 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
193 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
194 |     worklist.add(entry);
195 | 
196 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
197 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
198 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
199 |         continue;
200 |       }
201 | 
202 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
203 | 
204 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
205 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
206 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
207 |         worklist.add(candidate);
208 |       }
209 |     }
210 |   }

===== M0780 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1054 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1055 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1056 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1057 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1058 |       if (relationship != null) {
1059 |         ObjectType superClass = ObjectType.cast(
1060 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.superclassName));
1061 |         ObjectType subClass = ObjectType.cast(
1062 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.subclassName));
1063 |         if (superClass != null && subClass != null) {
1064 |           FunctionType superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1065 |           FunctionType subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1066 | 
1067 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS) {
1068 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1069 |           }
1070 | 
1071 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1072 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1073 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1074 |           }
1075 |         }
1076 |       }
1077 | 
1078 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1079 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1080 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1081 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1082 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1083 |         if (objectType != null) {
1084 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1085 | 
1086 |           if (functionType != null) {
1087 |             FunctionType getterType =
1088 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1089 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1090 |                 objectType);
1091 |           }
1092 |         }
1093 |       }
1094 | 
1095 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1096 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1097 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1098 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1099 |       }
1100 | 
1101 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1102 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
1103 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1104 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1105 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1106 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1107 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1108 |         } else {
1109 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1110 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1111 |         }
1112 |       }
1113 |     }

160 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
161 |     // Verify the nodes.
162 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
163 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);

165 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
166 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {

168 |       }
169 |     }
170 | 
171 |     return false;
172 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 416-468
Documentation:

Method code:
416 |     @Override
417 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
418 |       attachLiteralTypes(n);
419 | 
420 |       switch (n.getType()) {
421 |         case Token.CALL:
422 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
423 |           break;
424 | 
425 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
426 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
427 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
428 |           }
429 | 
430 |           // VARs and ASSIGNs are handled in different branches of this
431 |           // switch statement.
432 |           if (parent.getType() != Token.ASSIGN &&
433 |               parent.getType() != Token.NAME) {
434 |             defineDeclaredFunction(n, parent);
435 |           }
436 | 
437 |           break;
438 | 
439 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
440 |           // Handle constructor and enum definitions.
441 |           defineNamedTypeAssign(n, parent);
442 | 
443 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
444 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
445 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
446 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
447 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
448 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
449 |           }
450 |           break;
451 | 
452 |         case Token.CATCH:
453 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
454 |           break;
455 | 
456 |         case Token.VAR:
457 |           defineVar(n, parent);
458 |           break;
459 | 
460 |         case Token.GETPROP:
461 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
462 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
463 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
464 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
465 |           }
466 |           break;
467 |       }
468 |     }

===== M0812 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1675-1699
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1675 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1676 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1677 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1678 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1679 |             type.isUnknownType());
1680 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1681 |     if (info != null) {
1682 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1683 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1684 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1685 |         type = infoType;
1686 |       }
1687 | 
1688 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1689 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1690 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1691 |         compiler.report(
1692 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1693 |       }
1694 |     }
1695 | 
1696 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1697 |       n.setJSType(type);
1698 |     }
1699 |   }

===== M0819 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

===== M0824 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 443-455
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

===== M0829 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>,Collection<Var>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 114-154
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
114 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
115 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
116 |                 Scope functionScope,
117 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
118 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
119 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
120 |     this.compiler = compiler;
121 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
122 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
123 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
124 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
125 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
126 | 
127 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
128 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
129 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
130 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
131 |       }
132 |     }
133 | 
134 |     Iterator<Var> varIt = functionScope.getVars();
135 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
136 |       Var var = varIt.next();
137 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
138 |         continue;
139 |       }
140 | 
141 |       // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
142 |       // type is VOID.
143 |       if (var.getParentNode() != null &&
144 |           var.getType() == null && // no declared type
145 |           var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR &&
146 |           !var.isExtern()) {
147 |         this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
148 |             var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
149 |       }
150 |     }
151 | 
152 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
153 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));
154 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 117-138
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
117 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
118 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
119 |     String methodName = null;
120 |     if (callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
121 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
122 |     } else if (callName.getType() == Token.NAME) {
123 |       String name = callName.getString();
124 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
125 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
126 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
127 |       }
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     if (methodName != null) {
131 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
132 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
133 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
134 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
135 |       }
136 |     }
137 |     return null;
138 |   }

===== M0836 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 808-835
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
808 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
809 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
810 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
811 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
812 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
813 |           return;
814 |         }
815 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
816 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
817 | 
818 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
819 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
820 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
821 |           } else {
822 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
823 |           }
824 |         } else { // Has a catch.
825 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
826 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
827 |             return;
828 |           } else {
829 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
830 |           }
831 |         }
832 |         lastJump = handler;
833 |       }
834 |     }
835 |   }

===== M0844 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 479-525
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
479 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
480 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
481 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
482 |     switch (left.getType()) {
483 |       case Token.NAME:
484 |         String varName = left.getString();
485 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
486 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
487 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
488 |         }
489 | 
490 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
491 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
492 |         // This has two purposes:
493 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
494 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
495 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
496 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
497 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
498 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
499 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
500 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
501 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
502 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
503 |         }
504 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
505 |             resultType : null);
506 | 
507 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
508 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
509 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
510 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
511 |         }
512 |         break;
513 |       case Token.GETPROP:
514 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
515 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
516 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
517 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
518 |               resultType);
519 |         }
520 | 
521 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
522 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
523 |         break;
524 |     }
525 |   }

1170 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1171 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1172 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1173 | 
1174 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1175 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1176 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1177 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1178 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1179 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {

1183 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1184 |             return;
1185 |           }
1186 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1187 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1188 |           }
1189 |         }
1190 |       }
1191 | 
1192 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1193 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1194 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1195 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1196 |       // 4) ASSIGN to anything else
1197 |       //
1198 |       // 1 and 3 are declarations, 4 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1199 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1200 |       //
1201 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1202 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1203 | 
1204 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3
1205 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredGetPropType(t, info, n, rhsValue);
1206 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1207 |         // Determining type for #4
1208 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1209 |       }
1210 | 
1211 |       if (valueType == null) {
1212 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1213 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1214 |               n,
1215 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1216 |               ownerName));
1217 |         }
1218 | 
1219 |         return;
1220 |       }
1221 | 
1222 |       boolean inferred = true;
1223 |       if (info != null) {
1224 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3
1225 |         inferred = !(info.hasType() || info.hasEnumParameterType() ||
1226 |             FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1227 |       }
1228 | 
1229 |       if (inferred) {
1230 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1231 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1232 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1233 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1234 |       }
1235 | 
1236 |       if (!inferred) {
1237 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1238 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1239 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1240 |           // declared yet.
1241 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1242 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1243 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1244 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1245 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1246 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1247 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern);
1248 |           }
1249 |         }
1250 | 
1251 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1252 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1253 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1254 |       } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1255 |           rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {

1262 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1263 |           if (delegateType != null &&

1267 |           }
1268 |         }

===== M0872 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 212-287
Documentation:

Method code:
212 |   @Override
213 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
214 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
215 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
216 | 
217 |     switch (n.getType()) {
218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
219 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
220 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
221 |           return true;
222 |         }
223 |         return false;
224 |       case Token.TRY:
225 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |         return true;
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     /*
230 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
231 |      * is.
232 |      *
233 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
234 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
235 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
236 |      *
237 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
238 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
239 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
240 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
241 |      *
242 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
243 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
244 |      */
245 |     if (parent != null) {
246 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
247 |         case Token.FOR:
248 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
249 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
250 | 
251 |         // Skip the conditions.
252 |         case Token.IF:
253 |         case Token.WHILE:
254 |         case Token.WITH:
255 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
256 |         case Token.DO:
257 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
258 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
259 |         case Token.SWITCH:
260 |         case Token.CASE:
261 |         case Token.CATCH:
262 |         case Token.LABEL:
263 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
264 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
265 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
266 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
267 |         case Token.BREAK:
268 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
269 |         case Token.VAR:
270 |         case Token.RETURN:
271 |         case Token.THROW:
272 |           return false;
273 |         case Token.TRY:
274 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
275 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
276 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
277 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
278 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
279 |            */
280 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
281 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
282 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
283 |           }
284 |       }
285 |     }
286 |     return true;
287 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 751-781
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
751 |   @Override
752 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
753 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
754 |       return true;
755 |     }
756 | 
757 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
758 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
759 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
760 |         return true;
761 |       }
762 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
763 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
764 |         return false;
765 |       }
766 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
767 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
768 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
769 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
770 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
771 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
772 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
773 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
774 |       return (this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
775 |               other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
776 |               this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis)) &&
777 |           this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
778 |     }
779 | 
780 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
781 |   }

/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
408 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
409 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
410 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
411 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
412 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(
413 |           info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
414 |     }
415 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
416 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
417 |       thisType.setValidator(new ThisTypeValidator());
418 |     } else if (owner != null &&
419 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
420 |       // If the function is of the form:
421 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
422 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
423 |       // if it's of the form:
424 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
425 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
426 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();

428 |           typeRegistry.getForgivingType(
429 |               scope, ownerTypeName, sourceName,
430 |               owner.getLineno(), owner.getCharno()));
431 |       if (ownerType != null) {
432 |         thisType = ownerType;
433 |       }
434 |     }
435 | 
436 |     return this;
437 |   }
